# Strategy x scenario atlas

Sharpe of daily excess returns per scenario state (state known at the prior close). ✓ = Sharpe >= 0.5 in both halves of the strategy's history; ✗ = <= -0.3 in both halves. Daily strategies start 2007-06 (later where the ETF is younger); intraday streams 2019+.
